[부모 노트: ROMS NetCDF I/O](roms_io_netcdf.md).

# ROMS NetCDF I/O — def_·wrt_·get_·nf_ 계열 파일 구조와 병렬 I/O — 문서와 코드 차이 (10-14 대조)

## 문서와 코드 차이

문서 판은 ROMS 위키 wikitext이다. 문서는 2026-05-03에 받았다. 쪽별 revid는 [manifest.json](../raw/manuals/wiki/manifest.json)에 있다. 코드 판은 원본 저장소 HEAD `57aecf58`이다. 코드 날짜는 2026-09-18이다([출력 보고서 §1](../../../_staging/recovery/compare/roms-outputs-metadata-vs-code.md#1-요약)). 코드는 roms-4.2 이후 4.3 개발 판이다(`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:653` `      character (len=5  )    :: version = '4.3  '`). 표의 `roms_io_netcdf.md:줄` 인용은 결론 출처 보고서의 수정안 절에 보존된 수정 전 노트의 문장과 줄 번호를 가리킨다. [10-14 전수 검증 보완]

| build-options-history:RBH-2163 | mod_ncparam.F | `Modules.wiki:59` <code>&lt;section begin=mod_ncparam.F /&gt;;&lt;span id="mod_ncparam.F"&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&#91;&#91;mod_ncparam.F&#93;&#93;</code><br>`Modules.wiki:60` <code>:This contains all sorts of parameters relating to the NetCDF I/O files, including that read from the '''varinfo.dat''' file. The parameter &#91;&#91;Variables#MT \| MT&#93;&#93; is set here, giving the maximum number of variables that can be read.&lt;section end=mod_ncparam.F /&gt;</code> | `ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:101` `!  Maximum number of variables in a generic NetCDF file (MV) and`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:102` `!  maximum number of variables in information arrays (NV).`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:104` `      integer, parameter :: MV = 1400`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:105` `      integer, parameter :: NV = 1400`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:1694` `      MT=MAX(1,MAX(NAT,MAXVAL(NT)))`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:870` `!  Determine metadata file extension: 'varinfo.dat'  or`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:871` `!                                     'varinfo.yaml'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:943` `!  Otherwise, open deprecated 'varinfo.dat' file.` | B 문서 오기 | `NetCDF` 최대 변수 수를 `MV`·`NV`로 설명한다. `MT`는 추적자 배열 크기와 구분한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-2195 | External_Libraries.wiki 외부 절차 29 | `External_Libraries.wiki:29` <code>ROMS uses '''NetCDF''' for all its input and output data management. Its '''NetCDF3''' or '''NetCDF4''' type files can be processed using the standard library developed and distributed &#91;http://doi.org/10.5065/D6H70CW6 UCAR/Unidata&#93;, the Parallel-IO ('''PIO''') developed at ANL/NCAR (&#91;https://doi.org/10.1177/1094342011428143 Dennis ''et al''., 2012&#93;; &#91;https://www.researchgate.net/publication/348169990_THE_PARALLELIO_PIO_CFORTRAN_LIBRARIES_FOR_SCALABLE_HPC_PERFORMANCE Hartnett and Edwards, 2021&#93;). We have migrated to the Fortran-90 '''API''' interface,  which consists of a library and one or two module files. See the &#91;&#91;makefile&#93;&#93; and &#91;&#91;build_roms\| build script&#93;&#93; for how to tell ROMS where these are located. You will have to use a copy of the library and module files that were compiled using the same compiler you are using to compile ROMS - this is especially true for the module files.</code> | `ROMS/Modules/mod_pio_netcdf.F:3` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:160` `#ifdef SEAICE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:171` `#ifdef BIOLOGY`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:182` `#ifdef SEDIMENT`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:509` `#ifdef FLOATS`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:531` `#ifdef STATIONS`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_pio_netcdf.F:27` `      USE mod_netcdf`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_pio_netcdf.F:32` `      USE pio`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:166` `      OPEN (15, FILE=TRIM(iparnam), FORM='formatted', STATUS='old')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:177` `      OPEN (25, FILE=TRIM(bparnam), FORM='formatted', STATUS='old')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:188` `      OPEN (35, FILE=TRIM(sparnam), FORM='formatted', STATUS='old')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:515` `      OPEN (45, FILE=TRIM(fposnam), FORM='formatted', STATUS='old')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_par.F:537` `      OPEN (55, FILE=TRIM(sposnam), FORM='formatted', STATUS='old')` | B 문서 오기 | 모형 배열 자료의 `NetCDF` `I/O`와 텍스트 입력 스크립트의 `formatted` 읽기를 구분한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-3418 | Modules 목록에 없는 mod_extract.F | `Modules.wiki:3` `Now that we are using Fortran 90, the method of choice for managing data structures are modules. The '''ROMS/Modules''' directory contains all of the ROMS modules that contain globally used variables. The complete list is:` | `ROMS/Modules/mod_extract.F:2` `      MODULE mod_extract`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_extract.F:4` `#ifdef GRID_EXTRACT` | C 문서 누락 | `Modules` 위키 목록에는 `mod_extract.F`가 없다. `mod_extract`의 실제 내용은 `GRID_EXTRACT` 조건 아래에 있다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-3420 | Modules 목록에 없는 mod_pio_netcdf.F | `Modules.wiki:3` `Now that we are using Fortran 90, the method of choice for managing data structures are modules. The '''ROMS/Modules''' directory contains all of the ROMS modules that contain globally used variables. The complete list is:` | `ROMS/Modules/mod_pio_netcdf.F:2` `      MODULE mod_pio_netcdf`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_pio_netcdf.F:3` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE` | C 문서 누락 | `Modules` 위키 목록에는 `mod_pio_netcdf.F`가 없다. 이 모듈의 구현은 `PIO_LIB`와 `DISTRIBUTE`가 함께 정의된 조건에 속한다. |
| build-options-history:RBH-3504 | IO 노트의 모든 .F include 주장 | `roms_io_netcdf.md:20` ``ROMS .F 파일은 모두 C-preprocessor 매크로(`#include "cppdefs.h"`)로 시작하며, NetCDF 표준 라이브러리와 NCAR **PIO(Parallel-IO)** 라이브러리를 동일 이름의 generic interface로 추상화한다.`` | `ROMS/Utility/yaml_parser.F:1` `      MODULE yaml_parser_mod`<br>`ROMS/Utility/yaml_parser.F:10` `!  This module contains several routines to process input YAML files.  !`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_env.F:1` `      MODULE get_env_mod`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_env.F:10` `!  This module retrieves and decodes system environmental variables    !`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_env.F:11` `!  using Fortran 2003 GET_ENVIRONMENT_VARIABLE intrinsic function.     !` | B 문서 오기 | `ROMS`의 많은 수치 커널과 `I/O` 파일은 `cppdefs.h`를 포함한다. 독립 유틸리티에는 예외가 있다. |
| callflow-da:RF2-013 | 최종 분석 초기 상태 저장 | `RBL4D-Var_Tutorial.wiki:68` `plot the surface initial conditions increments`<br>`R4DVAR_Tutorial.wiki:62` | `ROMS/Drivers/rbl4dvar_roms.h:360–371` `IF (exit_flag.eq.NoError) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Drivers/r4dvar_roms.h:383–399` `CALL get_state (ng, iRPM, 1, TLM(ng), InpRec, Tindex)`<br>`ROMS/Drivers/i4dvar_roms.h:349–360`<br>`ROMS/Drivers/r4dvar_roms.h:362–409` | C 문서 누락 | 세 종료 루틴은 오류 없이 종료할 때 다음 동화 주기의 초기 상태로 사용할 `DAI`를 저장한다. `R4`는 최종 외부 반복의 마지막 `RPM` 기록을 먼저 읽는다. 이 `DAI` 출력은 동화 창 시작 시점의 증분을 그리는 절차와 구별한다. |
| callflow-da:RF2-025 | DAI를 daily 또는 daily average로 표시 | `roms_io_netcdf.md:34` `dai(daily-avg)`<br>`roms_io_netcdf.md:40` `def_dai`<br>`roms_io_netcdf.md:40` `(daily)` | `ROMS/Utility/def_dai.F:3` `#if defined FOUR_DVAR \|\| defined ENKF_RESTART`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_dai.F:12–13` `! | callflow-da:RF2-029 | DAI 저장의 IOtype 선택 대상 | — | `ROMS/Utility/wrt_dai.F:68` `SELECT CASE (DIA(ng)%IOtype)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/wrt_dai.F:79` `IF (Master) WRITE (stdout,10) DAI(ng)%IOtype`<br>`ROMS/Utility/wrt_dai.F:128` `DAI(ng)%Rindex=DAI(ng)%Rindex+1`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_dai.F:67` `SELECT CASE (DAI(ng)%IOtype)`<br>`ROMS/Drivers/i4dvar_roms.h:349–360` `IF (exit_flag.eq.NoError) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/wrt_dai.F:3`<br>`ROMS/Utility/wrt_dai.F:69`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4159–4162,4203–4206` | E 코드 결함 후보 (중간) | `DAI` 쓰기 함수는 `DIA`의 `IOtype`을 검사한다. 두 객체가 같은 `IOtype`이면 잘못된 선택 대상이 가려진다. 모든 실행의 입출력 실패로 확대하지 않는다. `E`를 유지한다. 근거 강도는 중간이다. |

| coupling-nesting-parallel:RCN-829 | nDataFiles의 그룹 수 | `coupling_esmf.in.wiki:258` `:The number of NetCDF files that the DATA Model needs to process and read exporting fields.`<br>`coupling_esmf.in.wiki:264` <code>:NetCDF filenames containing the field to export to specified ESM component, &#91;&#91;Variables#nDataFiles\|nDataFiles&#93;&#93; are expected. The order of files in the list is essential. See detailed information about multi-files &#91;&#91;Input Parameter Files\|here&#93;&#93;.</code> | `Master/mod_esmf_esm.F:2353` `DataSet(Icomp)%Nfiles=nD2R(1)`<br>`Master/mod_esmf_esm.F:2382` `Npts=load_s2d(Nval, Cval, Cdim, line, label, ifile,     &`<br>`Master/mod_esmf_esm.F:3091` `CALL load_IFS (D2R, nD2R(1), 1, nD2R, Iroms)`<br>`ESM/coupling_esmf.in:44–60` `nDataFiles(seaice)`<br>`ESM/coupling_esmf.in:44–60` `5 and not 10`<br>`ESM/coupling_esmf.in:57` | B 문서 오기 | `nDataFiles`에는 실제 파일 이름 수가 아니라 다중 파일 그룹 수를 쓴다. |
| coupling-nesting-parallel:RCN-831 | DATA 중첩 격자별 입력과 복제 | `coupling_esmf.in.wiki:268` `If applicable, the USER has the option to enter several sets of filenames for each nested grid.  Alternatively, if all the export fields are the same for each nesting grid, and the data is in its native resolution, we could enter only one set of filenames, and the DATA model will replicate those files internally to the remaining grids using the plural KEYWORD protocol. The USER may have different filenames for each export field.`<br>`coupling_esmf.in.wiki:270` <code>The DATA model will scan the files and will read the needed data from the first file in the list containing the export field. Therefore, the order of the filenames is critical. If using multiple files per nested grid, first enter all the filenames for grid one followed by two, and so on. It is also possible to split input data time records into several files (see &#91;&#91;Input Parameter Files&#93;&#93;). Use a single line per entry with a continuation (\) or a vertical bar (\|) symbol after each entry, except the last one.</code> | `Master/mod_esmf_esm.F:2383` `&                        igrid, 1, nD2R, Ncount, nD2R(1), inp_lib, &`<br>`Master/mod_esmf_esm.F:2378–2384` `DataFiles(roms)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:2383` `!     Mgrids     Number of nested grids (integer)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/inp_decode.F:2365–2366` | B 문서 오기 | 현재 `DataFiles(roms)` 판독은 `load_s2d`의 격자 수 인수에 1을 전달한다. 위키의 격자별 별도 파일 집합·복제 설명을 이 판독기의 기능으로 적용하지 않는다. |

| inputs:RI1-0871 | 위키 압축 옵션·MPI·parallel 금지 | `roms.in.wiki:221` <code>* NetCDF-4/HDF5 compression parameters for output files. The user needs to compile with the NetCDF-4/HDF5 and MPI libraries. File deflation cannot be used in parallel I/O for writing libraries. File deflation cannot be used in parallel I/O for writing to exactly map the data to the disk location.  For more information, check &#91;http://www.unidata.ucar.edu/software/netcdf NetCDF official website&#93;.&lt;div class="box"&gt;  &#91;&#91;Variables#shuffle\|NC_SHUFFLE&#93;&#93; =  1                 ! if non-zero, turn on shuffle filter&lt;br&gt;  &#91;&#91;Variables#deflate\|NC_DEFLATE&#93;&#93; =  1                 ! if non-zero, turn on deflate filter&lt;br&gt;   &#91;&#91;Variables#dlevel\|NC_DLEVEL&#93;&#93; =  1                 ! deflate level &#91;0-9&#93;&lt;/div&gt;</code><br>`IO.wiki:15` <code>Serial I/O is the standard option that has been in ROMS since the beginning. In this setup, all input and output data flows through the master parallel process. All data for output is collected from all processes by the master process and written to disk. Likewise, all data for input is read by the master process and distributed to the rest of the processes. When using serial I/O, files can be written in NetCDF classic/64-bit offset (NetCDF-3) or NetCDF-4/HDF5 (&#91;&#91;Options#HDF5\|HDF5&#93;&#93; CPP option) formats. | inputs:RI1-0882 | IO quicksave single record | `IO.wiki:44` `<span class="mediumOrchid">(daily, single record files)</span>`<br>`IO.wiki:51` <code>where the Fortran intrinsic function '''MOD(&lt;span class="red"&gt;X&lt;/span&gt;,&lt;span class="red"&gt;Y&lt;/span&gt;)''' computes the remainder of the division of &lt;span class="red"&gt;X&lt;/span&gt; by &lt;span class="red"&gt;Y&lt;/span&gt;, and has to be always '''zero''' for ROMS multi-file option to work. Notice that the first files in the &lt;span class="twilightBlue"&gt;history&lt;/span&gt; data series (&lt;span class="violet"&gt;roms_his_0001.nc&lt;/span&gt;) will contain '''5'''-time records because of the initial conditions, and the rest of the files will have '''4'''-time records. Similarly, the first file in the &lt;span class="twilightBlue"&gt;quicksave&lt;/span&gt; series (&lt;span class="violet"&gt;roms_qck_0001.nc&lt;/span&gt;) will contain '''9'''-time records, and the rest will have '''8'''-time records. The time-averaged data in the &lt;span class="twilightBlue"&gt;averages&lt;/span&gt; and &lt;span class="twilightBlue"&gt;diagnostic&lt;/span&gt; files are single records files representing daily averaged fields. The above analysis also holds when converted to time in seconds since every parameter is multiplied by the &#91;&#91;Variables#dt\|DT&#93;&#93; timestep.</code> | `ROMS/Nonlinear/output.F:423` `IF (MOD(iic(ng)-1,nQCK(ng)).eq.0) THEN` | B 문서 오기 | 이 예제의 `quicksave`는 첫 파일 9개 레코드와 이후 파일별 8개 레코드를 기록한다. |

| inputs:RI1-0900 | IO NTIMES 불변 의무 | `IO.wiki:53` <code>&lt;span class="red"&gt;WARNING:&lt;/span&gt; If running your application in a supercomputer with limited-time job queues, long simulations will require a restart. If &#91;&#91;Variables#dstart\|DSTART&#93;&#93; is changed, it will reset the timestep internal counter &lt;span class="red"&gt;iic(ng)&lt;/span&gt;, and we are in deep trouble! Notice that the value &#91;&#91;Variables#nRST\|NRST&#93;&#93; is crucial for the restart of ROMS with multi-file. | inputs:RI1-0983 | OUT_LIB 보고 분기의 입력 변수 검사 | `roms.in.wiki:201` <code>* Choose the input and output NetCDF library to use. For example, the user could choose to use the PIO library for writing but still use the standard library for reading. &lt;span class="twilightBlue"&gt;To use this Parallel I/O strategy, the '''PIO''' library must be linked to ROMS at compile time and the &#91;&#91;PIO_LIB&#93;&#93; CPP option needs to be activated. It is only available in distributed-memory applications since it uses MPI-IO.&lt;/span&gt; &lt;div class="box"&gt;!   &#91;1&#93; Standard NetCDF-3 or NetCDF-4 library&lt;br /&gt;!   &#91;2&#93; Parallel-IO from the PIO library (MPI, MPI-IO applications)&lt;br /&gt;&lt;br /&gt;     &#91;&#91;Variables#INP_LIB\|INP_LIB&#93;&#93; =  2&lt;br /&gt;     &#91;&#91;Variables#OUT_LIB\|OUT_LIB&#93;&#93; =  2&lt;/div&gt;</code><br>`User/External/roms.in:3196` `! INP_LIB      Reading library flag for input NetCDF files`<br>`User/External/roms.in:3197` `!`<br>`User/External/roms.in:3198` `! OUT_LIB      Creation/writing library flag for output NetCDF files` | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3980` `out_lib=Ivalue(1)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4174` `&                      Ngrids, Nfiles, out_lib, HIS)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7509` `IF (out_lib.eq.io_nf90) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7512` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7513` `ELSE IF (inp_lib.eq.io_pio) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7514` `WRITE (out,120) out_lib, 'out_lib',                         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7515` `&            'Using Parallel-IO (PIO) library for output files.'`<br>`68c7aa40:ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:6358` `          IF (out_lib.eq.io_nf90) THEN`<br>`68c7aa40:ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:6362` `          ELSE IF (inp_lib.eq.io_pio) THEN`<br>`68c7aa40:ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:6363` `            WRITE (out,120) out_lib, 'out_lib',                         &`<br>`68c7aa40:ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:6364` `     &            'Using Parallel-IO (PIO) library for output files.'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7509` `          IF (out_lib.eq.io_nf90) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7513` `          ELSE IF (inp_lib.eq.io_pio) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7514` `            WRITE (out,120) out_lib, 'out_lib',                         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7515` `     &            'Using Parallel-IO (PIO) library for output files.'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4174` `     &                      Ngrids, Nfiles, out_lib, HIS)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:5011` `IF (Master.and.Lwrite) THEN` | E 코드 결함 후보 (강함) | `PIO_LIB`와 `DISTRIBUTE`에서 `OUT_LIB` 보고 분기가 `inp_lib`를 검사하는 결함 후보가 있다. 이 문제의 범위는 `IF (Master.and.Lwrite) THEN` 안의 출력 라이브러리 보고이다. 근거 강도는 강함이다. |
| inputs:RI1-0989 | 동·북 성분 동시 출력 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4577` `IF (.not.Hout(idu2dE,ng).and.Hout(idv2dN,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4578` `Hout(idu2dE,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4580` `IF (.not.Hout(idv2dN,ng).and.Hout(idu2dE,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4581` `Hout(idv2dN,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4583` `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4584` `IF (.not.Hout(idu3dE,ng).and.Hout(idv3dN,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4585` `Hout(idu3dE,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4587` `IF (.not.Hout(idv3dN,ng).and.Hout(idu3dE,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4588` `Hout(idv3dN,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4591` `#ifdef AVERAGES`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4592` `IF (.not.Aout(idu2dE,ng).and.Aout(idv2dN,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4593` `Aout(idu2dE,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4595` `IF (.not.Aout(idv2dN,ng).and.Aout(idu2dE,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4596` `Aout(idv2dN,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4598` `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4599` `IF (.not.Aout(idu3dE,ng).and.Aout(idv3dN,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4600` `Aout(idu3dE,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4602` `IF (.not.Aout(idv3dN,ng).and.Aout(idu3dE,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4603` `Aout(idv3dN,ng)=.TRUE.` | C 문서 누락 | `Hout`와 `Aout`의 동·북 성분 중 하나를 켜면 다른 성분도 켠다. `Aout` 보정은 `AVERAGES`에서 존재한다. 3`D` 보정은 `SOLVE3D`에서 존재한다. |
| inputs:RI1-0990 | HISTORY·QUICKSAVE 파일 활성 조건 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4615` `IF ((nHIS(ng).gt.0).and.ANY(Hout(:,ng))) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4616` `LdefHIS(ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4619` `#ifdef GRID_EXTRACT`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4623` `IF ((nXTR(ng).gt.0).and.ANY(Hout(:,ng))) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4624` `LdefXTR(ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4630` `IF ((nQCK(ng).gt.0).and.ANY(Qout(:,ng))) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4631` `LdefQCK(ng)=.TRUE.` | C 문서 누락 | 파일을 활성화하려면 출력 간격이 양수이고 해당 출력 스위치 중 하나가 `TRUE`여야 한다. `GRID_EXTRACT`의 `XTR`도 `Hout` 목록을 사용한다. |
| inputs:RI1-0997 | 출력 기간 부족·0 간격 해제 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4806` `IF (((nrrec(ng).eq.0).and.(nAVG(ng).gt.ntimes(ng))).or.         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4807` `&      (nAVG(ng).eq.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4808` `LdefAVG(ng)=.FALSE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4814` `IF (((nrrec(ng).eq.0).and.(nFLT(ng).gt.ntimes(ng))).or.         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4815` `&      (nFLT(ng).eq.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4816` `LdefFLT(ng)=.FALSE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4818` `IF (((nrrec(ng).eq.0).and.(nHIS(ng).gt.ntimes(ng))).or.         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4819` `&      (nHIS(ng).eq.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4820` `LdefHIS(ng)=.FALSE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4822` `IF (((nrrec(ng).eq.0).and.(nQCK(ng).gt.ntimes(ng))).or.         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4823` `&      (nQCK(ng).eq.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4824` `LdefQCK(ng)=.FALSE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4826` `IF (((nrrec(ng).eq.0).and.(nRST(ng).gt.ntimes(ng))).or.         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4827` `&      (nRST(ng).eq.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4828` `LdefRST(ng)=.FALSE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4830` `IF (((nrrec(ng).eq.0).and.(nSTA(ng).gt.ntimes(ng))).or.         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4831` `&      (nSTA(ng).eq.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4832` `LdefSTA(ng)=.FALSE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4810` `IF (((nrrec(ng).eq.0).and.(nDIA(ng).gt.ntimes(ng))).or.         &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4811` `&      (nDIA(ng).eq.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4812` `LdefDIA(ng)=.FALSE.` | C 문서 누락 | 새 실행에서 출력 간격이 `ntimes`보다 크면 파일 정의 스위치를 끈다. 간격 0도 파일 정의 스위치를 끈다. `AVG`·`DIA`·`FLT`·`HIS`·`QCK`·`RST`·`STA`에 적용한다. |
| inputs:RI1-0998 | 평균·진단 잔여 파일 수 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4910` `#ifdef AVERAGES`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4911` `IF ((nAVG(ng).gt.0).and.(ndefAVG(ng).gt.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4912` `OutFiles=ntimes(ng)/ndefAVG(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4913` `IF ((nAVG(ng).eq.ndefAVG(ng)).or.                             &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4914` `&        (MOD(ntimes(ng),ndefAVG(ng)).ge.nAVG(ng))) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4915` `OutFiles=Outfiles+1`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4917` `CALL edit_file_struct (ng, OutFiles, AVG)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4918` `AVG(ng)%load=0       ! because delayed creation of NetCDF file`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4921` `#ifdef DIAGNOSTICS`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4922` `IF ((nDIA(ng).gt.0).and.(ndefDIA(ng).gt.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4923` `OutFiles=ntimes(ng)/ndefDIA(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4924` `IF ((nDIA(ng).eq.ndefDIA(ng)).or.                             &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4925` `&        (MOD(ntimes(ng),ndefDIA(ng)).ge.nDIA(ng))) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4927` `END IF`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4928` `CALL edit_file_struct (ng, OutFiles, DIA)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4929` `DIA(ng)%load=0       ! because delayed creation of NetCDF file` | C 문서 누락 | `AVERAGES`·`DIAGNOSTICS`의 파일 수는 정수 몫과 잔여 출력으로 정한다. 파일 정의를 지연하므로 `load=0`으로 초기화한다. |
| inputs:RI1-1011 | Z_SLICE 부호 보정·순서 비검사 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3941` `CASE ('Z_SLICE')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3942` `IF (Nslice.gt.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3943` `Npts=load_r(Nval, Rval, Nslice, Zslice)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3944` `Zslice=-ABS(Zslice)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3945` `DO i=1,Nslice`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3946` `IF (Zslice(i).gt.0.0_r8) THEN` | C 문서 누락 | `NSLICE>0`이면 모든 입력을 -`ABS`로 바꾼다. 이 뒤의 양수 검사는 이 보정값을 대상으로 한다. 깊이 순서는 검사하지 않는다. |
| inputs:RI1-1012 | INP_LIB·OUT_LIB 비-PIO 강제값 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3965` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3974` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3975` `inp_lib=1`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3979` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3988` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3989` `out_lib=1` | C 문서 누락 | `PIO_LIB&&DISTRIBUTE`를 함께 켜지 않으면 두 입력을 1로 바꾼다. 문서의 `PIO` 선택은 이 `CPP` 조합에서만 적용된다. |
| inputs:RI1-1013 | 파일별 라이브러리 예외 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4226` `&                      Ngrids, Nfiles, io_nf90, INI)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4987` `BRY(:,ng)%IOtype=io_nf90            ! lateral boundary file(s)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4988` `SSF(ng)%IOtype=io_nf90              ! Sources/Sink forcing file`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4981` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE` | C 문서 누락 | `INI` 파일과 `GRX` 파일은 파일명 판독에서 `io_nf90`를 사용한다. `PIO_LIB&&DISTRIBUTE`를 켜면 `BRY`·`SSF` 파일의 `IOtype`을 `io_nf90`로 덮어쓴다. `INP_LIB`가 모든 파일 구조에 그대로 적용되지는 않는다. |
| inputs:RI1-1022 | PIO task 수·stride·base·aggregator 검사 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3994` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4025` `IF ((pio_NumIOtasks.lt.1).or.                             &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4026` `&            (pio_NumIOtasks.gt.numthreads)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4036` `IF ((pio_stride.lt.1).or.                                 &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4037` `(pio_stride*pio_NumIOtasks.gt.numthreads)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4047` `IF (pio_base.lt.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4054` `IF ((numthreads.eq.1).and.(pio_base.ne.0)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4058` `pio_base=0          ! reset since offset is not needed`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4064` `IF (pio_aggregator.lt.1) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4068` `exit_flag=5` | C 문서 누락 | `PIO_LIB&&DISTRIBUTE`에서 `I/O` `task`는 1..`numthreads`이다. `stride`는 양수이다. `stride*task` 수는 `numthreads`를 넘지 않아야 한다. `base`는 음수를 거부한다. 한 프로세스에서는 `base`를 0으로 바꾼다. `aggregator`는 양수이다. |
| inputs:RI1-1023 | PIO_METHOD 오류의 RETURN 부재 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3997` `IF ((Ivalue(1).lt.0).or.(Ivalue(1).gt.4)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3998` `IF (Master) WRITE (out,260) 'pio_method = ',            &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4000` `&                                      'Must be between 1 and 4'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4001` `ELSE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4020` `END SELECT`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4021` `END IF`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3994` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE` | C 문서 누락 | `PIO_LIB`와 `DISTRIBUTE`를 함께 정의한 구성의 처리이다. 0..4 밖이면 오류 메시지를 쓴다. 이 `IF`는 `exit_flag` 설정과 `RETURN`을 두지 않는다. 메시지는 1..4라고 적지만 실제 검사는 0..4를 사용한다. 대상 문서는 오류 이후 처리를 적지 않는다. |
| inputs:RI1-1024 | PIO pending 음수 상수 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4129` `CASE ('PIO_C2I_Preq')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4131` `IF (Ivalue(1).lt.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4132` `pio_rearr_C2I_PR=PIO_rearr_comm_unlimited_pend_req`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4134` `pio_rearr_C2I_PR=Ivalue(1)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4142` `CASE ('PIO_I2C_Preq')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4144` `IF (Ivalue(1).lt.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4145` `pio_rearr_I2C_PR=PIO_rearr_comm_unlimited_pend_req`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:4147` `pio_rearr_I2C_PR=Ivalue(1)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3994` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE` | C 문서 누락 | `PIO_LIB`와 `DISTRIBUTE`를 함께 정의한 구성의 처리이다. 음수 최대 `pending` 요청 수는 `PIO_rearr_comm_unlimited_pend_req`로 바꾼다. `C2I`와 `I2C`에 모두 적용한다. |
| inputs:RI1-1025 | 출력 metadata ID 검사 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1436` `CASE ('Hout(idFsur)')`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1437` `IF (idFsur.eq.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1438` `IF (Master) WRITE (out,280) 'idFsur'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1439` `exit_flag=5`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1440` `RETURN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1441` `END IF`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1442` `Npts=load_l(Nval, Cval, Ngrids, Lswitch)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1443` `Hout(idFsur,1:Ngrids)=Lswitch(1:Ngrids)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3605` `IF (M3hjvf.le.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:3607` `exit_flag=5` | C 문서 누락 | 여러 `Hout`·`Dout` 분기는 `metadata` 변수 `ID=0` 또는 `diagnostic` 항 번호<=0이면 `exit_flag=5`로 반환한다. 스위치의 최종 `T`·`F`를 대입하기 전에 검사한다. 각 키워드 행에 인용한 `IF`도 같은 계열의 검사이다. |
| inputs:RI1-1032 | station 수·좌표 제한·검사 순서 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:523` `IF (.not.Lstations(ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:524` `Nstation(ng)=0`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:526` `IF (Nstation(ng).le.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:538` `allocate ( SCALARS(ng) % Sflag(Nstation(ng)) )`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:546` `READ (inp,*,ERR=10,END=10) igrid, flag, Xpos, Ypos`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:547` `ng=MAX(1,MIN(ABS(igrid),Ngrids))`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:549` `is(ng)=is(ng)+1`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:550` `SCALARS(ng)%Sflag(is(ng))=flag`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:556` `IF (Lstations(ng).and.(Nstation(ng).ne.is(ng))) THEN` | C 문서 누락 | 비활성 격자의 `NSTATION`은 0으로 바꾼다. 활성 격자는 양수를 요구한다. `GRID`는 `ABS` 뒤 1..`Ngrids`로 제한한다. 좌표 저장 뒤 `EOF` 또는 `READ` 오류에서 지정 수와 실제 수를 비교한다. 좌표 저장 전의 `station` 배열 상한 검사는 없다. |
| inputs:RI1-1033 | station 출력이 없으면 처리 해제 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:579` `IF (.not.LdefSTA(ng).and.Lstations(ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:580` `Lstations(ng)=.FALSE.` | C 문서 누락 | `LdefSTA`가 `FALSE`이면 `Lstations`도 `FALSE`로 바꾼다. |
| inputs:RI1-1034 | station 동·북 성분 동시 활성 | — | `ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:588` `IF (.not.Sout(idu2dE,ng).and.Sout(idv2dN,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:589` `Sout(idu2dE,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:591` `IF (.not.Sout(idv2dN,ng).and.Sout(idu2dE,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:592` `Sout(idv2dN,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:594` `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:595` `IF (.not.Sout(idu3dE,ng).and.Sout(idv3dN,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:596` `Sout(idu3dE,ng)=.TRUE.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:598` `IF (.not.Sout(idv3dN,ng).and.Sout(idu3dE,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_stapar.F:599` `Sout(idv3dN,ng)=.TRUE.` | C 문서 누락 | 한 방향 `Sout`만 `TRUE`여도 대응 성분을 `TRUE`로 만든다. `SOLVE3D`에서 3`D` 성분에도 적용한다. |
| inputs:RI1-1042 | 현재 노트: nf90·PIO 분기 | `roms_io_netcdf.md:147` ``- `PARALLEL_IO && DISTRIBUTE` 빌드(`nf_fwrite2d.F:86-`): 각 프로세스가 PIO 로 자기 타일을 직접 write. `pack_field2d` 로 타일 데이터 패킹 (`:519-520`) 후 `nf90_put_var(ncid, ncvarid, Awrk, start, total)` (`:551`).``<br>`roms_io_netcdf.md:148` ``- 비-PARALLEL_IO 분산 빌드(`nf90_fwrite2d`, `:89-`): `mp_collect` 로 전 타일을 마스터에 모은 뒤 단일 write.``<br>`roms_io_netcdf.md:149` ``- 일반 격자(`gtype>0`): `nf90_put_var(ncid, ncvarid, Awrk, start, total)` (`:295`).``<br>`roms_io_netcdf.md:150` ``- water-point 압축(`gtype<0`): `CALL mp_collect (ng, model, Npts, IniVal, Awrk)` (`:356`) → `nf90_put_var(ncid, ncvarid, Awrk(Istr:), start, total)` (`:379`).`` | `ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:79` `#if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:80` `MODULE PROCEDURE pio_fwrite2d`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:86` `#if defined PARALLEL_IO && defined DISTRIBUTE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:286` `!  Write out data: all parallel nodes write their own packed tile data.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:295` `status=nf90_put_var(ncid, ncvarid, Awrk, start, total)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:394` `#else`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:550` `IF (OutThread) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fwrite2d.F:551` `status=nf90_put_var(ncid, ncvarid, Awrk, start, total)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/pack_field.F:713` `CALL mp_gather2d (ng, model, LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj,                &` | B 문서 오기 | `PARALLEL_IO&&DISTRIBUTE`에서 `nf90_fwrite2d`는 각 타일을 `nf90_put_var`로 쓴다. 이를 `PIO` `backend`라고 부르지 않는다. 그 외 표준 경로는 `pack_field2d`에서 자료를 모으고 `OutThread`가 쓴다. `PIO_LIB&&DISTRIBUTE`의 `pio_fwrite2d`는 별도 `backend`이다. |
| inputs:RI1-1044 | 현재 노트: read_phypar 총 줄 수 | `roms_io_netcdf.md:178` `` `read_phypar.F:11` *"This routine reads and reports physical model input parameters."* — `roms.in` 형식 키워드 입력을 파싱(8237 라인). 하위 분야별: `` | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:8243` `END SUBROUTINE read_PhyPar` | B 문서 오기 | `read_phypar.F`는 `ROMS` `ASCII` 키워드를 읽고 보고한다. 총 줄 수 대신 코드 `HEAD` 57`aecf58`과 해당 `CASE` 줄을 기록한다. |
| inputs:RI1-1071 | regrid 대상의 2D 조건 | `Input_Parameter_Files.wiki:29` `If the horizontal number of grid points for a forcing field is different than the grid domain dimensions, it will automatically trigger reggriding inside ROMS after the forcing data is read.` | `ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:222` `!  Determine if interpolating from coarse gridded data to model grid`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:223` `!  is required.  This is only allowed for gridded 2D fields.  This is`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:229` `IF (((Vsize(1).gt.0).and.(Vsize(1).ne.Isize)).or.                 &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:230` `&    ((Vsize(2).gt.0).and.(Vsize(2).ne.Jsize))) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:222–230` | C 문서 누락 | 수평 크기 불일치에 따른 `regrid` 설명은 `gridded` 2`D` `forcing` 경로의 조건과 함께 기록한다. |
| mixing-bc-forcing:RMF1-780 | CDL Eposition attribute 대상 | `River_Runoff.wiki:66` `double river_Eposition(river) ;<br/>		river_Eposition:long_name = "river ETA-position" ;<br/>                river_Xposition:LuvSrc_True_meaning = "j-index grid-cell of u- or v-face source/sink" ;<br/>                river_Xposition:LwSrc_True_meaning = "j-index grid-cell of w-face source/sink" ;<br/>	` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/get_idata.F:755–761` `        CALL get_ngfld (ng, iNLM, idRepo, SSF(ng)%ncid,                 &<br># if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE<br>     &                  SSF(ng)%pioFile,                                &<br># endif<br>     &                  1, SSF(ng), recordless, update(1),              &<br>     &                  1, Nsrc(ng), 1, 1, 1, Nsrc(ng), 1,              &<br>     &                  SOURCES(ng) % Ysrc)`<br>`Data/ROMS/CDL/frc_rivers.cdl:27–32` `	double river_Eposition(river) ;<br>		river_Eposition:long_name = "river ETA-position at RHO-points" ;<br>		river_Eposition:valid_min = 1. ;<br>		river_Eposition:valid_max = 129. ;<br>		river_Eposition:LuvSrc_True_meaning = "j-index grid cell of U- or V-face source/sink" ;<br>		river_Eposition:LwSrc_True_meaning = "j-index grid cell of W-face source/sink" ;` | B 문서 오기 | 문서는 `j-index`의 두 `attribute`를 `river_Xposition`에 붙였다. 현재 `CDL`은 `river_Eposition`에 붙인다. |
| mixing-bc-forcing:RMF1-792 | river_flag 제거·호환 | `LtracerSrc.wiki:8–10` `This logical switch '''replaces''' and '''eliminates''' the need to have or read the variable <span class="forestGreen">river_flag(river)</span> from the input rivers forcing NetCDF file:<div class="box">  double river_flag(river)<br />          river_flag:long_name = "river runoff tracer flag"<br />          river_flag:option_0 = "all tracers are off"<br />          river_flag:option_1 = "only temperature"<br />          river_flag:option_2 = "only salinity"<br />          river_flag:option_3 = "both temperature and salinity"<br />          river_flag:units = "nondimensional"</div><br><br>The above variable was too cumbersome and complicated when additional tracers are considered. However, this change is backward compatible.` | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:1083–1092` `            CASE ('LtracerSrc')<br>              Npts=load_l(Nval, Cval, NAT+NPT, Ngrids, Ltracer)<br>              DO ng=1,Ngrids<br>                DO itrc=1,NAT<br>                  LtracerSrc(itrc,ng)=Ltracer(itrc,ng)<br>                END DO<br># ifdef T_PASSIVE<br>                DO i=1,NPT<br>                  itrc=inert(i)<br>                  LtracerSrc(itrc,ng)=Ltracer(NAT+i,ng)`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/get_data.F:122–130` `      DO i=1,NT(ng)<br>        IF (LtracerSrc(i,ng)) THEN<br>          CALL get_ngfld (ng, iNLM, idRtrc(i), SSF(ng)%ncid,            &<br>#  if defined PIO_LIB && defined DISTRIBUTE<br>     &                    SSF(ng)%pioFile,                              &<br>#  endif<br>     &                    1, SSF(ng), recordless, update(1),            &<br>     &                    1, Nsrc(ng), N(ng), 2, 1, Nsrc(ng), N(ng),    &<br>     &                    SOURCES(ng) % TsrcG(:,:,:,i))` | C 문서 누락 | `river_flag`를 포함한 옛 파일도 추가 변수를 남길 수 있다. `LtracerSrc`는 별도로 지정해야 한다. |
| mixing-bc-forcing:RMF1-861 | river index 반올림·범위 제한 | `River_Runoff.wiki:97` `When using option <span class="red">LuvSrc = T</span>, <span class="blue">river_Xposition</span> and <span class="blue">river_Eposition</span> refer to the <span class="red">(i,j)</span> index of the '''u'''-face or '''v'''-face the flow crosses - NOT the <span class="red">(i,j)</span> index of the '''ρ'''-cell it flows into. The <span class="red">(i,j)</span> values must follow ROMS Fortran numbering convention for the appropriate '''u'''-point or '''v'''-point on the ROMS staggered grid.` | `ROMS/Nonlinear/get_idata.F:784–788` `        DO is=1,Nsrc(ng)<br>          SOURCES(ng)%Isrc(is)=                                         &<br>     &                MAX(1,MIN(NINT(SOURCES(ng)%Xsrc(is)),Lm(ng)+1))<br>          SOURCES(ng)%Jsrc(is)=                                         &<br>     &                MAX(1,MIN(NINT(SOURCES(ng)%Ysrc(is)),Mm(ng)+1))` | C 문서 누락 | `river` `position`을 `NINT`로 반올림한다. `Isrc`를 1:`Lm+1`로 제한한다. `Jsrc`를 1:`Mm+1`로 제한한다. 잘못된 좌표를 오류로 거부하는 경로는 이 대입에 없다. |
| mixing-bc-forcing:RMF1-868 | wet mask output fill 처리 | `WET_DRY.wiki:14` <code>* For output, a variable &#91;&#91;Variables#rmask_wet&amp;#124;wet_dry_masking&#93;&#93; provides the time history of the wet/dry masking at &amp;rho;-points.</code> | `ROMS/Utility/wrt_his.F:288–300` `!  Write out wet/dry mask at RHO-points.<br>!<br>      scale=1.0_dp<br>      gtype=gfactor*r2dvar<br>      status=nf_fwrite2d(ng, model, HIS(ng)%ncid, idRwet,               &<br>     &                   HIS(ng)%Vid(idRwet),                           &<br>     &                   HIS(ng)%Rindex, gtype,                         &<br>     &                   LBi, UBi, LBj, UBj, scale,                     &<br># ifdef MASKING<br>     &                   GRID(ng) % rmask,                              &<br># endif<br>     &                   GRID(ng) % rmask_wet,                          &<br>     &                   SetFillVal = .FALSE.)` | C 문서 누락 | `rho` `mask`를 `history` `record`에 쓴다. `SetFillVal=.FALSE`.로 `mask`의 `dry` 값도 보존한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M008 | varinfo.dat.wiki:14 | `varinfo.dat.wiki:14` `        Vinfo(7)      Staggered C-grid variable type:`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:16` `                            'p2dvar' => 2D PHI-variable.` | `ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1553` `          CASE ('p2dvar')`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1554` `            gtype=p2dvar`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1889` `              text='lon_psi lat_psi'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1888` `            ELSE IF (INT(Aval(5)).eq.p2dvar) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1890` `              location='node'`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:716` `      integer, parameter :: p2dvar = 1         ! 2D PSI-variable` | B 문서 오기 | 문서의 `p2dvar` 설명에 있는 `PHI`는 `PSI`의 오기다. `def_var`는 `p2dvar`의 좌표를 `lon_psi`와 `lat_psi`로 지정한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M012 | varinfo.dat.wiki:14 | `varinfo.dat.wiki:14` `        Vinfo(7)      Staggered C-grid variable type:`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:20` `                            'p3dvar' => 3D PHI-variable.` | `ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1561` `          CASE ('p3dvar')`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1562` `            gtype=p3dvar`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1889` `              text='lon_psi lat_psi'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1891` `            ELSE IF (INT(Aval(5)).eq.p3dvar) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1892` `              IF (has_z_slice) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1893` `                text='lon_psi lat_psi z_slice'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1894` `              ELSE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1895` `                text='lon_psi lat_psi s_rho'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1896` `              END IF`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:1897` `              location='node'`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_param.F:720` `      integer, parameter :: p3dvar = 5         ! 3D PSI-variable` | B 문서 오기 | 문서의 `p3dvar` 설명에 있는 `PHI`는 `PSI`의 오기다. `def_var`는 `p3dvar`의 수평 좌표를 `lon_psi`와 `lat_psi`로 지정한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M018 | varinfo.dat.wiki:3 | `varinfo.dat.wiki:3` `The varinfo.dat file specifies many things about the input/output rules for ROMS. Read the comments at the beginning of varinfo.dat for an introduction to the meaning of the various entries Vinfo(1) through Vinfo(7) and scale. `<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:5` `  This file contains all ROMS/TOMS Input/Output variables information. It is used to manage NetCDF files. `<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:6` `  The following information is needed for any IO variable:`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:26` `        scale         Scale to convert data to model input units.`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:28` `    Enclose Vinfo variables with single quotes so this file can be read with a free format statement. The Vinfo(6) variable must be    `<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:29` `    unique and case sensitive. It is checked in "mod_ncparm" to set the appropiate ID index for the information arrays.` | `ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1003` `          READ (iunit,*,ERR=10,END=20) Vinfo(1)  ! variable`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1012` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(8)       ! index code`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1013` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(7)       ! C-grid type`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1014` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) scale`<br>`roms-3.9:ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1155` `          READ (inp,*,ERR=30) Vinfo(5)`<br>`roms-3.9:ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1156` `          READ (inp,*,ERR=30) Vinfo(6)`<br>`roms-3.9:ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1157` `          READ (inp,*,ERR=30) Vinfo(7)`<br>`roms-3.9:ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1158` `          READ (inp,*,ERR=30) scale`<br>`d2530aaa:ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1008` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(5)       ! associated time`<br>`d2530aaa:ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1009` `            Vinfo(6)='nulvar'                    ! standard_name`<br>`d2530aaa:ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1010` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(8)       ! index code`<br>`d2530aaa:ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1011` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(7)       ! C-grid type`<br>`d2530aaa:ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1012` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) scale` | A 판 차이 | 과거 `DAT` 판독기는 여섯째 입력 문자열을 `Vinfo(6)`에 읽었다. `d2530aaa` 이후 `DAT`의 여섯째 문자열은 `Vinfo(8)`의 `index` `code`이다. 현재 `Vinfo(6)`은 `standard_name` 자리이다. (`git` 판정: `d2530aaa`(2022-02-27)) |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M024 | varinfo.dat.wiki:59 | `varinfo.dat.wiki:59` `If you change the scale factor in your varinfo.dat, I recommend you change the units string in Vinfo(2) to be consistent with your data file, and insert a comment to remind yourself what you’ve done in using a new scale factor. You can append a comment using a ! after anyentry in varinfo.dat.` | `ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1007` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(2)       ! long_name`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1008` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(3)       ! units` | B 문서 오기 | `units`는 `DAT` 세 번째 문자열 `Vinfo(3)`이다. 문서의 `Vinfo(2)`는 `long_name`이다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M025 | varinfo.dat.wiki:64 | `varinfo.dat.wiki:64` ` Index     Default NetCDF  Long Name               Units required      Other     Scale`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:65` ` Vinfo(5)  variable name                           Internally by ROMS  units             ` | `ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1010` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(5)       ! associated time`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1012` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(8)       ! index code` | B 문서 오기 | 표의 `Index` 열은 `DAT`의 여섯째 입력 문자열이다. 현행 `reader`는 이 문자열을 `Vinfo(8)`에 저장한다. `Vinfo(5)`는 시간 변수 이름이다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M041 | varinfo.dat.wiki:128 | `varinfo.dat.wiki:128` `  'ONES_west'`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:129` `   'ammonium western boundary condition'`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:130` `   'millimole_nitrogen meter-3'`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:131` `   'NH4_east, scalar, series'`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:132` `   'NH4_time'`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:133` `   'idTbry(iwest,iNH4_)'`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:134` `   'nulvar'`<br>`varinfo.dat.wiki:135` `   0.1d0          ! make all values equal to 0.1 millimole meter-3` | `ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1003` `          READ (iunit,*,ERR=10,END=20) Vinfo(1)  ! variable`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1007` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(2)       ! long_name`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1008` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(3)       ! units`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1009` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(4)       ! field`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1010` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(5)       ! associated time`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1012` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(8)       ! index code`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1013` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) Vinfo(7)       ! C-grid type`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:1014` `            READ (iunit,*,ERR=10) scale` | B 문서 오기 | 서쪽 암모늄 경계 예제의 입력 변수 이름은 `ONES_west`이다. `field` 문자열의 `NH4_east`는 서쪽 경계 설명과 어긋난다. `reader`는 `field`를 `Vinfo(4)`에 저장한다. 경계 `index` `code`인 `idTbry(iwest`,`iNH4_)`는 별도 슬롯 `Vinfo(8)`에 저장한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M055 | varinfo.yaml.wiki:79 | `varinfo.yaml.wiki:79` `:<span class="twilightBlue">ROMS_internal_units</span> = (your_units) x (<span class="violet">scale</span>) + <span class="violet">add_offset</span>`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:103` `\| align="center" \| idTair`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:104` `\| align="center" \| Tair`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:105` `\| surface air temperature`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:106` `\| align="center" \| Celsius`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:107` `\| align="center" \| Kelvin`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:108` `\| align="center" \| -273.15d0`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:109` `\| align="center" \| 1.0d0`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:68` `* <span class="violet">add_offset</span> is the value to be added to input NetCDF data. Currently, not used.`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:69` `* <span class="violet">scale</span> is the factor to multiply input data for units conversion purposes.` | `ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1546` `        Ldone = io_metadata(FirstPass, Vinfo, scale, add_offset)`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2656` `            Iinfo(1,varid,ng)=gtype`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2657` `            Fscale(varid,ng)=scale`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2652` `          DO i=1,6`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2653` `            Vname(i,varid)=TRIM(ADJUSTL(Vinfo(i)))`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2654` `          END DO`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2655` `          DO ng=1,Ngrids`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2658` `          END DO`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:244` `!  If the "add_offset" attribute is present, this value is added to the`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:245` `!  data after reading.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:246` `!  If both "scale_factor" and "add_offset" attributes are present, the`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:247` `!  data are first scaled before the offset is added.` | B 문서 오기 | 문서의 + `add_offset`과 `Kelvin` 변환 예제는 현재 메타데이터 경로에 적용되지 않는다. 같은 쪽 68줄은 `add_offset`을 사용하지 않는다고 명시한다. `NetCDF` 파일의 `add_offset` 속성을 처리하는 별도 경로와 구분해야 한다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M066 | varinfo.yaml.wiki:178 | `varinfo.yaml.wiki:178` `If you specify '''sustr''' as input and output (which you can do: ROMS will read forcing '''sustr''' from a forcing NetCDF file and write '''sustr''' to history, quicksave, averages, and stations.) even if you do a '''scale''' factor conversion you’d better keep the <span class="violet">scale</span> keyword units as '''Newton meter-2''' so that the output files have the correct metadata in the NetCDF attributes.` | `ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:907` `          IF (FoundError(yaml_get(YML, 'metadata.units',                &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:939` `          IF (FoundError(yaml_get(YML, 'metadata.scale',                &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:974` `          Vinfo(3)=Ystring3(Ientry)%value  ! 'units'         key`<br>`ROMS/Utility/get_metadata.F:981` `          scale   =Yreal2(Ientry)          ! 'scale'         key` | B 문서 오기 | `Newton` `meter-2`는 `units` 키에 넣어야 한다. `scale` 키는 실수 곱셈 계수다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-M081 | YAML 격자 w3dvar | `varinfo.yaml.wiki:53` `\| <span class="mediumOrchid">w3dvar</span>`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:54` `\| <span class="twilightBlue">3D W-variable, grid cell y-edges</span>` | `ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1569` `          CASE ('w3dvar')`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1570` `            gtype=w3dvar`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:824` `              ELSE IF (INT(Aval(5)).eq.w3dvar) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_var.F:825` `                text='lon_rho lat_rho s_w'`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/set_depth.F:176` `              Hz(i,j,k)=z_w(i,j,k)-z_w(i,j,k-1)` | B 문서 오기 | `W`격자는 수직 셀 경계의 `w` 지점이다. 문서의 `cell` `y-edges`는 `v`격자의 설명이다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-SN098 | dye_&lt;nn&gt; standard_name | `varinfo.yaml.wiki:497` `\| <span class="mediumOrchid">dye_&lt;nn&gt;</span>`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:498` `\| <span class="twilightBlue">mass_concentration_of_dye_&lt;nn&gt;_in_sea_water</span>` | `ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:3295` `  - variable:       dye_                                             # Input/Output`<br>`ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:3296` `    standard_name:  mass_concentration_of_dye_`<br>`ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:3301` `    index_code:     idTvar(inert(i))`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2660` `#ifdef T_PASSIVE`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2665` `            CASE ('idTvar(inert(i))')`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2715` `                DO i=1,NPT`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2722` `                  WRITE (Vname(1,varid),'(a,i2.2)')                     &`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2723` `     &                  TRIM(ADJUSTL(Vinfo(1))), i`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2732` `                  WRITE (Vname(6,varid),'(a,i2.2)')                     &`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2733` `     &                  TRIM(ADJUSTL(Vinfo(6))), i`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2668` `# ifdef AGE_MEAN`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2688` `                  WRITE (Vname(6,varid),'(a,i2.2)')                     &`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2689` `     &                  TRIM(ADJUSTL(Vinfo(6))), ic`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2666` `              IF (NPT.gt.0) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2670` `                DO i=1,NPT,2`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2672` `                  ic=ic+1`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2714` `# else` | B 문서 오기 | 생성 조건은 `T_PASSIVE`가 있고 `NPT`가 양수인 경우다. `standard_name`은 `mass_concentration_of_dye_` 뒤에 두 자리 번호를 붙인다. 코드는 위키에 적힌 `_in_sea_water`를 덧붙이지 않는다. `AGE_MEAN`의 일반 `dye` 분기는 `ic`를 번호로 사용한다. `AGE_MEAN`이 없으면 `i`를 번호로 사용한다. |

| outputs-metadata:OMD-P027 | YAML 헤더 38 | `ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:38` `#  An input field is processed as:`<br>`ROMS/External/varinfo.yaml:40` `#     field = field (source_units) * scale + add_offset`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:68` `* <span class="violet">add_offset</span> is the value to be added to input NetCDF data. Currently, not used.`<br>`varinfo.yaml.wiki:69` `* <span class="violet">scale</span> is the factor to multiply input data for units conversion purposes.` | `ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:1546` `        Ldone = io_metadata(FirstPass, Vinfo, scale, add_offset)`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2656` `            Iinfo(1,varid,ng)=gtype`<br>`ROMS/Modules/mod_ncparam.F:2657` `            Fscale(varid,ng)=scale`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:252` `      AttName(1)='scale_factor'`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:253` `      AttName(2)='add_offset  '`<br>`ROMS/Utility/nf_fread2d.F:370` `              wrk(i)=Ascl*(Afactor*wrk(i)+Aoffset)` | B 문서 오기 | `YAML` `scale`은 `Fscale`에 저장된다. `YAML` `add_offset`은 현재 입력 필드에 적용하지 않는다. 따라서 배포 `YAML` 헤더의 + `add_offset` 설명은 현재 동작과 다르다. 위키는 `add_offset`이 현재 미사용임을 명시한다. `NetCDF` 변수의 `packing` 속성 `add_offset`은 별도 입력 경로에서 적용된다. |

| outputs-metadata:OMD-D058 | Model_Diagnostics:6 | `Model_Diagnostics.wiki:6` <code>The tables below show the variables that are written to the &#91;&#91;Variables#DIAname\|diagnostics output file&#93;&#93;.</code> | `ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:491` `# ifdef DIAGNOSTICS_UV`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:574` `# ifdef DIAGNOSTICS_TS`<br>`ROMS/Nonlinear/set_avg.F:210` `      IF (Aout(id2dPV,ng).or.Aout(id2dRV,ng).or.                        &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_avg.F:1034` `        IF (Aout(id2dPV,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_avg.F:1053` `        IF (Aout(id2dRV,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_avg.F:1070` `# ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_avg.F:1074` `        IF (Aout(id3dPV,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_avg.F:1093` `        IF (Aout(id3dRV,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_his.F:1055` `#ifdef SOLVE3D`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_his.F:1289` `        IF (Hout(id3dPV,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_his.F:1308` `        IF (Hout(id3dRV,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:603` `# ifdef DIAGNOSTICS_BIO`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:604` `#  if defined BIO_FENNEL \|\| defined ECB \|\| defined HYPOXIA_SRM`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:605` `!`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:606` `!  Define 2D biological diagnostic fields.`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:607` `!`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:608` `        DO ivar=1,NDbio2d`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:609` `          ifield=iDbio2(ivar)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:610` `          IF (Dout(ifield,ng)) THEN` | B 문서 오기 | `DIA`는 활성화된 운동량·트레이서·생물 진단 중 `Dout`으로 선택된 항을 정의한다. 와도 목록은 해당 `Aout`·`Hout` 조건의 `AVG`·`HIS` 변수이며 3`D` 와도는 `SOLVE3D`를 요구한다. 모든 표를 `DIAname` 출력으로 소개한 문서의 범위가 잘못됐다. |
| outputs-metadata:OMD-D061 | Model_Diagnostics:161 | `Model_Diagnostics.wiki:161` `{{note}}The diagnostics code adapts to the model configuration, the terms marked with * are written to the diagnostics output file when appropriate. Diagnostic terms are reported for all tracers.` | `ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:578` `        DO itrc=1,NT(ng)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:580` `            ifield=idDtrc(itrc,ivar)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:581` `            IF (Dout(ifield,ng)) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:574` `# ifdef DIAGNOSTICS_TS`<br>`ROMS/Utility/def_diags.F:579` `          DO ivar=1,NDT` | C 문서 누락 | `DIAGNOSTICS_TS` 경로는 모든 `NT(ng)` 트레이서와 `NDT` 진단 항을 순회한다. 실제 변수 정의는 `Dout(ifield`,`ng)`이 참인 항에 한정된다. 위키의 모든 트레이서 진단 설명은 이 선택 조건을 빠뜨린다. |

Wednesday - January 30, 2008 -  4:07:42 PM` | `roms-3.0:ROMS/Utility/close_io.F:156` ` 10   FORMAT (/,' ROMS/TOMS - Output NetCDF summary for Grid ',         &`<br>`roms-3.0:ROMS/Utility/close_io.F:161` ` 40   FORMAT (/,' ROMS/TOMS: DONE... ',a)`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/close_io.F:573` ` 10   FORMAT (/,' ROMS - Output NetCDF summary for Grid ',              &`<br>`57aecf58:ROMS/Utility/close_io.F:581` ` 60   FORMAT (/,' ROMS: DONE... ',a)` | A 판 차이 | `ROMS` 3.0의 출력 요약과 종료 문구는 `ROMS/TOMS`를 사용한다. `f3cd98c7(2024-12-27)` 변경 뒤 두 문구는 `ROMS`를 사용한다. |

| outputs-metadata:OMD-SO100 | 표준 출력 예제 119 | `Standard_Output.wiki:119` `    IO Variable Information File:  External/varinfo.dat` | `ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7971` `        fname=varname`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7972` `        IF (.not.find_file(ng, out, fname, 'VARNAME')) THEN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7973` `          IF (FoundError(exit_flag, NoError, __LINE__, MyFile)) RETURN`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7974` `        ELSE`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7975` `          IF (Master.and.Lwrite) WRITE (out,230)                        &`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7976` `     &      'ROMS I/O variables Metadata File:  ', TRIM(fname)`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:7977` `        END IF`<br>`ROMS/Utility/read_phypar.F:8207` ` 230  FORMAT (2x,a,a)` | F 표기 차이 | 현재 항목명은 `ROMS` `I/O` `variables` `Metadata` `File`이다. 파일명은 `VARNAME` 입력값이며 `DAT`와 `YAML`을 모두 선택할 수 있다. 이 값과 경로는 2008년 `BENCHMARK`의 출력 예제다. 코드 기본값 또는 현재 실행 결과로 해석하지 않는다. |
